  120→    left_post = left_times[(left_times >= go_time) & (left_times <= stop_time)]\\n   121→    right_post = right_times[(right_times >= go_time) & (right_times <= stop_time)]\\n   122→    post_choice = first_side(left_post, right_post)\\n   123→    if post_choice is not None:\\n   124→        return post_choice\\n   125→\\n   126→    left_any = left_times[(left_times >= start_time) & (left_times <= stop_time)]\\n   127→    right_any = right_times[(right_times >= start_time) & (right_times <= stop_time)]\\n   128→    any_choice = first_side(left_any, right_any)\\n   129→    if any_choice is not None:\\n   130→        return any_choice\\n   131→    return instructed_choice\\n   132→\\n   133→\\n   134→def clean_tongue_tracking(\\n   135→    x: np.ndarray,\\n   136→    y: np.ndarray,\\n   137→    likelihood: np.ndarray,\\n   138→) -> tuple[np.ndarray, dict]:\\n   139→    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()\\n   140→    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()\\n   141→    likelihood = np.asarray(likelihood, dtype=np.float64)\\n   142→\\n   143→    speed = np.zeros_like(x)\\n   144→    if len(x) > 1:\\n   145→        speed[1:] = np.sqrt(np.diff(x) ** 2 + np.diff(y) ** 2)\\n   146→    speed_threshold = float(np.nanmean(speed) + 5.0 * np.nanstd(speed))\\n   147→    outlier_mask = ~np.isfinite(x) | ~np.isfinite(y) | (speed > speed_threshold)\\n   148→\\n   149→    frame_idx = np.arange(len(x), dtype=np.float64)\\n   150→    keep_mask = ~outlier_mask\\n   151→    if np.sum(keep_mask) >= 2:\\n   152→        x[outlier_mask] = np.interp(frame_idx[outlier_mask], frame_idx[keep_mask], x[keep_mask])\\n   153→        y[outlier_mask] = np.interp(frame_idx[outlier_mask], frame_idx[keep_mask], y[keep_mask])\\n   154→    else:\\n   155→        x[outlier_mask] = np.nanmean(x)\\n   156→        y[outlier_mask] = np.nanmean(y)\\n   157→\\n   158→    visible_mask = np.isfinite(likelihood) & (likelihood >= TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESHOLD)\\n   159→    mean_y = float(np.nanmean(y[visible_mask])) if np.any(visible_mask) else float(np.nanmean(y))\\n   160→    occluded_mask = ~visible_mask\\n   161→    y[occluded_mask] = mean_y\\n   162→\\n   163→    diagnostics = {\\n   164→        \\\"speed_threshold\\\": speed_threshold,\\n   165→        \\\"n_velocity_outliers\\\": int(np.sum(outlier_mask)),\\n   166→        \\\"n_low_likelihood_frames\\\": int(np.sum(occluded_mask)),\\n   167→        \\\"mean_visible_y\\\": mean_y,\\n   168→    }\\n   169→    return y, diagnostics\\n   170→\\n   171→\\n   172→def align_tongue_y(\\n   173→    timestamps: np.ndarray,\\n   174→    cleaned_y: np.ndarray,\\n   175→    go_times: np.ndarray,\\n   176→) -> np.ndarray:\\n   177→    abs_centers = go_times[:, None] + REL_CENTERS[None, :]\\n   178→    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, abs_centers, side=\\\"right\\\") - 1\\n   179→    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, len(timestamps) - 1)\\n   180→    return cleaned_y[idx]\\n   181→\\n   182→\\n   183→def build_choice_array(\\n   184→    trial_instruction: np.ndarray,\\n   185→    outcome_code: np.ndarray,\\n   186→    start_times: np.ndarray,\\n   187→    go_times: np.ndarray,\\n   188→    stop_times: np.ndarray,\\n   189→    left_lick_times: np.ndarray,\\n   190→    right_lick_times: np.ndarray,\\n   191→) -> np.ndarray:\\n   192→    choice = np.zeros(len(trial_instruction), dtype=np.int16)\\n   193→    instructed = np.where(trial_instruction == \\\"left\\\", 0, 1).astype(np.int16)\\n   194→\\n   195→    hit_mask = outcome_code == 2\\n   196→    miss_mask = outcome_code == 1\\n   197→    ignore_mask = outcome_code == 0\\n   198→\\n   199→    choice[hit_mask] = instructed[hit_mask]\\n   200→    choice[miss_mask] = 1 - instructed[miss_mask]\\n   201→\\n   202→    ignore_trials = np.where(ignore_mask)[0]\\n   203→    for trial_idx in ignore_trials:\\n   204→        choice[trial_idx] = lick_choice_with_fallback(\\n   205→            left_times=left_lick_times,\\n   206→            right_times=right_lick_times,\\n   207→            start_time=float(start_times[trial_idx]),\\n   208→            go_time=float(go_times[trial_idx]),\\n   209→            stop_time=float(stop_times[trial_idx]),\\n   210→            instructed_choice=int(instructed[trial_idx]),\\n   211→        )\\n   212→    return choice\\n   213→\\n   214→\\n   215→def build_photostim_matrix(\\n   216→    photostim_onset_str: np.ndarray,\\n   217→    photostim_duration_str: np.ndarray,\\n   218→    start_times: np.ndarray,\\n   219→    go_times: np.ndarray,\\n   220→) -> np.ndarray:\\n   221→    onset_trial = parse_optional_float_array(photostim_onset_str)\\n   222→    duration = parse_optional_float_array(photostim_duration_str)\\n   223→    go_minus_start = go_times - start_times\\n   224→    onset_rel_go = onset_trial - go_minus_start\\n   225→    offset_rel_go = onset_rel_go + duration\\n   226→\\n   227→    stim = np.zeros((len(go_times), N_BINS), dtype=np.float16)\\n   228→    valid = np.isfinite(onset_rel_go) & np.isfinite(offset_rel_go)\\n   229→    for trial_idx in np.where(valid)[0]:\\n   230→        mask = (REL_CENTERS >= onset_rel_go[trial_idx]) & (REL_CENTERS < offset_rel_go[trial_idx])\\n   231→        stim[trial_idx, mask] = 1.0\\n   232→    return stim\\n   233→\\n   234→\\n   235→def bin_spikes_to_firing_rates(\\n   236→    spike_times_flat: np.ndarray,\\n   237→    spike_times_index: np.ndarray,\\n   238→    good_unit_indices: np.ndarray,\\n   239→    trial_edges_abs: np.ndarray,\\n   240→) -> np.ndarray:\\n   241→    n_trials = trial_edges_abs.shape[0]\\n   242→    n_edges = trial_edges_abs.shape[1]\\n   243→    flat_edges = trial_edges_abs.reshape(-1)\\n   244→    start_index = np.concatenate(([0], spike_times_index[:-1]))\\n   245→    firing_rates = np.empty((len(good_unit_indices), n_trials, n_edges - 1), dtype=np.float16)\\n   246→\\n   247→    for i, unit_idx in enumerate(good_unit_indices):\\n   248→        spikes = spike_times_flat[start_index[unit_idx]: spike_times_index[unit_idx]]\\n   249→        edge_idx = np.searchsorted(spikes, flat_edges, side=\\\"left\\\").reshape(n_trials, n_edges)\\n   250→        counts = np.diff(edge_idx, axis=1)\\n   251→        firing_rates[i] = (counts / BIN_SIZE_S).astype(np.float16)\\n   252→    return firing_rates\\n   253→\\n   254→\\n   255→def compute_valid_trial_mask(\\n   256→    obs_intervals: np.ndarray,\\n   257→    go_times: np.ndarray,\\n   258→) -> np.ndarray:\\n   259→    trial_start = go_times + REL_START_S\\n   260→    trial_end = go_times + REL_END_S\\n   261→    starts = obs_intervals[:, 0][None, :]\\n   262→    ends = obs_intervals[:, 1][None, :]\\n   263→    return np.any((trial_start[:, None] >= starts) & (trial_end[:, None] <= ends), axis=1)\\n   264→\\n   265→\\n   266→def make_session_plot(\\n   267→    result: SessionResult,\\n   268→    out_path: Path,\\n   269→) -> None:\\n   270→    diag = result.diagnostics\\n   271→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(3, 2, figsize=(16, 12))\\n   272→\\n   273→    trial_idx = int(diag[\\\"example_trial_idx\\\"])\\n   274→    neural = result.neural_trials[trial_idx]\\n   275→    show_neurons = min(40, neural.shape[0])\\n   276→    ax[0, 0].imshow(neural[:show_neurons], aspect=\\\"auto\\\", cmap=\\\"viridis\\\", interpolation=\\\"nearest\\\")\\n   277→    ax[0, 0].set_title(f\\\"Neural FR Heatmap ({show_neurons} neurons, trial {trial_idx})\\\")\\n   278→    ax[0, 0].set_xlabel(\\\"50 ms bins\\\")\\n   279→    ax[0, 0].set_ylabel(\\\"Neuron\\\")\\n   280→\\n   281→    ax[0, 1].plot(REL_CENTERS, result.input_trials[trial_idx][0], label=\\\"time_from_tone_onset_s\\\")\\n   282→    ax[0, 1].step(REL_CENTERS, result.input_trials[trial_idx][1], where=\\\"mid\\\", label=\\\"photostim_on\\\")\\n   283→    ax[0, 1].set_title(\\\"Inputs\\\")\\n   284→    ax[0, 1].legend(loc=\\\"upper left\\\")\\n   285→\\n   286→    ax[1, 0].plot(diag[\\\"tracking_preview_time\\\"], diag[\\\"tracking_preview_raw\\\"], alpha=0.6, label=\\\"raw tongue y\\\")\\n   287→    ax[1, 0].plot(diag[\\\"tracking_preview_time\\\"], diag[\\\"tracking_preview_clean\\\"], alpha=0.8, label=\\\"clean tongue y\\\")\\n   288→    ax[1, 0].set_title(\\\"Tongue Tracking Cleaning\\\")\\n   289→    ax[1, 0].legend(loc=\\\"upper right\\\")\\n   290→\\n   291→    ax[1, 1].plot(REL_CENTERS, diag[\\\"aligned_tongue_y_trial\\\"], label=\\\"aligned y\\\")\\n   292→    ax[1, 1].step(REL_CENTERS, result.output_trials[trial_idx][3], where=\\\"mid\\\", label=\\\"discrete class\\\")\\n   293→    ax[1, 1].set_title(\\\"Aligned Tongue Y\\\")\\n   294→    ax[1, 1].legend(loc=\\\"upper left\\\")\\n   295→\\n   296→    ax[2, 0].hist(diag[\\\"photostim_onsets_rel_go\\\"], bins=30)\\n   297→    ax[2, 0].set_title(\\\"Photostim Onset Relative to Go\\\")\\n   298→    ax[2, 0].set_xlabel(\\\"Seconds\\\")\\n   299→\\n   300→    outcome_vals = np.array([trial[1, 0] for trial in result.output_trials], dtype=np.int16)\\n   301→    choice_vals = np.array([trial[0, 0] for trial in result.output_trials], dtype=np.int16)\\n   302→    early_vals = np.array([trial[2, 0] for trial in result.output_trials], dtype=np.int16)\\n   303→    ax[2, 1].hist(\\n   304→        [choice_vals, outcome_vals, early_vals],\\n   305→        bins=np.arange(-0.5, 3.5, 1.0),\\n   306→        label=[\\\"choice\\\", \\\"outcome\\\", \\\"early\\\"],\\n   307→    )\\n   308→    ax[2, 1].set_title(\\\"Output Value Distributions\\\")\\n   309→    ax[2, 1].legend(loc=\\\"upper right\\\")\\n   310→\\n   311→    fig.suptitle(f\\\"Processing Diagnostics: {result.session_id}\\\")\\n   312→    fig.tight_layout()\\n   313→    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=150)\\n   314→    plt.close(fig)\\n   315→\\n   316→\\n   317→def process_session(path: Path) -> SessionResult:\\n   318→    t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n   319→    session_id = path.stem\\n   320→    subject_id = path.parent.name\\n   321→    print(f\\\"[session] {session_id} - loading\\\")\\n   322→\\n   323→    with h5py.File(path, \\\"r\\\") as f:\\n   324→        trials = f[\\\"intervals/trials\\\"]\\n   325→        n_trials_raw = len(trials[\\\"id\\\"])\\n   326→\\n   327→        classification = decode_str_array(f[\\\"units/classification\\\"])\\n   328→        good_mask = classification == \\\"good\\\"\\n   329→        good_unit_indices = np.flatnonzero(good_mask)\\n   330→        if len(good_unit_indices) == 0:\\n   331→            raise ValueError(f\\\"Session {session_id} has no good units\\\")\\n   332→\\n   333→        anno_name = decode_str_array(f[\\\"units/anno_name\\\"])\\n   334→        region_labels = anno_name[good_unit_indices]\\n   335→\\n   336→        start_times_all = trials[\\\"start_time\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   337→        stop_times_all = trials[\\\"stop_time\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   338→        go_times_all = f[\\\"acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times/timestamps\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   339→        if len(go_times_all) != n_trials_raw:\\n   340→            raise ValueError(f\\\"{session_id}: go cue count {len(go_times_all)} does not match trial count {n_trials_raw}\\\")\\n   341→\\n   342→        obs_intervals_index = f[\\\"units/obs_intervals_index\\\"][()]\\n   343→        obs_intervals = get_ragged_row(\\n   344→            f[\\\"units/obs_intervals\\\"],\\n   345→            obs_intervals_index,\\n   346→            int(good_unit_indices[0]),\\n   347→        )\\n   348→        valid_trial_mask = compute_valid_trial_mask(obs_intervals=obs_intervals, go_times=go_times_all)\\n   349→        if not np.any(valid_trial_mask):\\n   350→            raise ValueError(f\\\"{session_id}: no valid trials found from obs_intervals\\\")\\n   351→\\n   352→        start_times = start_times_all[valid_trial_mask]\\n   353→        stop_times = stop_times_all[valid_trial_mask]\\n   354→        go_times = go_times_all[valid_trial_mask]\\n   355→        n_trials = len(go_times)\\n   356→\\n   357→        trial_instruction = decode_str_array(trials[\\\"trial_instruction\\\"])[valid_trial_mask]\\n   358→        outcome_str = decode_str_array(trials[\\\"outcome\\\"])[valid_trial_mask]\\n   359→        early_lick_str = decode_str_array(trials[\\\"early_lick\\\"])[valid_trial_mask]\\n   360→        photostim_onset_str = decode_str_array(trials[\\\"photostim_onset\\\"])[valid_trial_mask]\\n   361→        photostim_duration_str = decode_str_array(trials[\\\"photostim_duration\\\"])[valid_trial_mask]\\n   362→\\n   363→        outcome_code = np.array([{\\\"ignore\\\": 0, \\\"miss\\\": 1, \\\"hit\\\": 2}[x] for x in outcome_str], dtype=np.int16)\\n   364→        early_code = np.array([{\\\"no early\\\": 0, \\\"early\\\": 1}[x] for x in early_lick_str], dtype=np.int16)\\n   365→\\n   366→        left_lick_times = f[\\\"acquisition/BehavioralEvents/left_lick_times/timestamps\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   367→        right_lick_times = f[\\\"acquisition/BehavioralEvents/right_lick_times/timestamps\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   368→        choice_code = build_choice_array(\\n   369→            trial_instruction=trial_instruction,\\n   370→            outcome_code=outcome_code,\\n   371→            start_times=start_times,\\n   372→            go_times=go_times,\\n   373→            stop_times=stop_times,\\n   374→            left_lick_times=left_lick_times,\\n   375→            right_lick_times=right_lick_times,\\n   376→        )\\n   377→\\n   378→        input_time = np.tile((REL_CENTERS - TONE_ONSET_REL_GO_S).astype(np.float16), (n_trials, 1))\\n   379→        input_stim = build_photostim_matrix(\\n   380→            photostim_onset_str=photostim_onset_str,\\n   381→            photostim_duration_str=photostim_duration_str,\\n   382→            start_times=start_times,\\n   383→            go_times=go_times,\\n   384→        )\\n   385→\\n   386→        tongue_ts = f[\\\"acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking\\\"]\\n   387→        tongue_data = tongue_ts[\\\"data\\\"][()]\\n   388→        tongue_x = tongue_data[:, 0]\\n   389→        tongue_y = tongue_data[:, 1]\\n   390→        tongue_likelihood = tongue_data[:, 2]\\n   391→        tongue_timestamps = tongue_ts[\\\"timestamps\\\"][()].astype(np.float64)\\n   392→        cleaned_tongue_y, tongue_clean_diag = clean_tongue_tracking(\\n   393→            x=tongue_x,\\n   394→            y=tongue_y,\\n   395→            likelihood=tongue_likelihood,\\n   396→        )\\n   397→        aligned_tongue_y = align_tongue_y(\\n   398→            timestamps=tongue_timestamps,\\n   399→            cleaned_y=cleaned_tongue_y,\\n   400→            go_times=go_times,\\n   401→        )\\n   402→\\n   403→        spike_times_flat = f[\\\"units/spike_times\\\"][()]\\n   404→        spike_times_index = f[\\\"units/spike_times_index\\\"][()]\\n   405→        trial_edges_abs = go_times[:, None] + REL_EDGES[None, :]\\n   406→        t_neural = time.perf_counter()\\n   407→        firing_rates = bin_spikes_to_firing_rates(\\n   408→            spike_times_flat=spike_times_flat,\\n   409→            spike_times_index=spike_times_index,\\n   410→            good_unit_indices=good_unit_indices,\\n   411→            trial_edges_abs=trial_edges_abs,\\n   412→        )\\n   413→        nonzero_trial_mask = np.any(firing_rates != 0, axis=(0, 2))\\n   414→        if not np.all(nonzero_trial_mask):\\n   415→            firing_rates = firing_rates[:, nonzero_trial_mask, :]\\n   416→            start_times = start_times[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   417→            stop_times = stop_times[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   418→            go_times = go_times[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   419→            trial_instruction = trial_instruction[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   420→            outcome_code = outcome_code[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   421→            early_code = early_code[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   422→            choice_code = choice_code[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   423→            photostim_onset_str = photostim_onset_str[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   424→            input_time = input_time[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   425→            input_stim = input_stim[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   426→            aligned_tongue_y = aligned_tongue_y[nonzero_trial_mask]\\n   427→            n_trials = len(go_times)\\n   428→        neural_time_s = time.perf_counter() - t_neural\\n   429→\\n   430→        q40, q60 = np.percentile(aligned_tongue_y.reshape(-1), [40, 60])\\n   431→        tongue_disc = np.ones(aligned_tongue_y.shape, dtype=np.int16)\\n   432→        tongue_disc[aligned_tongue_y < q40] = 0\\n   433→        tongue_disc[aligned_tongue_y > q60] = 2\\n   434→\\n   435→    neural_trials = [firing_rates[:, i, :].copy() for i in range(n_trials)]\\n   436→    del firing_rates\\n   437→    gc.collect()\\n   438→\\n   439→    input_trials = []\\n   440→    output_trials = []\\n   441→    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\\n   442→        input_trial = np.vstack([input_time[trial_idx], input_stim[trial_idx]]).astype(np.float16)\\n   443→        output_trial = np.vstack(\\n   444→            [\\n   445→                np.full(N_BINS, choice_code[trial_idx], dtype=np.int16),\\n   446→                np.full(N_BINS, outcome_code[trial_idx], dtype=np.int16),\\n   447→                np.full(N_BINS, early_code[trial_idx], dtype=np.int16),\\n   448→                tongue_disc[trial_idx].astype(np.int16),\\n   449→            ]\\n   450→        )\\n   451→        input_trials.append(input_trial)\\n   452→        output_trials.append(output_trial)\\n   453→\\n   454→    photostim_onsets_rel_go = []\\n   455→    onset_trial = parse_optional_float_array(photostim_onset_str)\\n   456→    go_minus_start = go_times - start_times\\n   457→    onset_rel_go = onset_trial - go_minus_start\\n   458→    photostim_onsets_rel_go = onset_rel_go[np.isfinite(onset_rel_go)]\\n   459→\\n   460→    preview_n = min(2000, len(tongue_timestamps))\\n   461→    diagnostics = {\\n   462→        \\\"tracking_preview_time\\\": tongue_timestamps[:preview_n] - go_times[0],\\n   463→        \\\"tracking_preview_raw\\\": tongue_y[:preview_n],\\n   464→        \\\"tracking_preview_clean\\\": cleaned_tongue_y[:preview_n],\\n   465→        \\\"aligned_tongue_y_trial\\\": aligned_tongue_y[0],\\n   466→        \\\"photostim_onsets_rel_go\\\": photostim_onsets_rel_go,\\n   467→        \\\"example_trial_idx\\\": 0,\\n   468→        \\\"q40\\\": float(q40),\\n   469→        \\\"q60\\\": float(q60),\\n   470→        \\\"n_trials\\\": int(n_trials),\\n   471→        \\\"n_trials_raw\\\": int(n_trials_raw),\\n   472→        \\\"n_trials_obs_valid\\\": int(np.sum(valid_trial_mask)),\\n   473→        \\\"n_good_units\\\": int(len(good_unit_indices)),\\n   474→        \\\"n_raw_units\\\": int(len(classification)),\\n   475→        \\\"binning_time_s\\\": float(neural_time_s),\\n   476→        \\\"session_time_s\\\": float(time.perf_counter() - t0),\\n   477→        **tongue_clean_diag,\\n   478→    }\\n   479→\\n   480→    print(\\n   481→        f\\\"[session] {session_id} - done \\\"\\n   482→        f\\\"({n_trials} trials, {len(good_unit_indices)} good units, \\\"\\n   483→        f\\\"{diagnostics['session_time_s']:.1f}s total, {neural_time_s:.1f}s binning)\\\"\\n   484→    )\\n   485→\\n   486→    return SessionResult(\\n   487→        session_id=session_id,\\n   488→        subject_id=subject_id,\\n   489→        neural_trials=neural_trials,\\n   490→        input_trials=input_trials,\\n   491→        output_trials=output_trials,\\n   492→        region_names=sorted(set(region_labels.tolist())),\\n   493→        region_labels_per_unit=region_labels.copy(),\\n   494→        n_raw_units=int(len(classification)),\\n   495→        n_good_units=int(len(good_unit_indices)),\\n   496→        n_trials=int(n_trials),\\n   497→        diagnostics=diagnostics,\\n   498→    )\\n   499→\\n   500→\\n   501→def build_dataset(results: list[SessionResult]) -> dict:\\n   502→    subjects = []\\n   503→    subject_to_idx = {}\\n   504→    brain_regions = []\\n   505→    region_to_idx = {}\\n   506→\\n   507→    data = {\\n   508→        \\\"neural\\\": [],\\n   509→        \\\"input\\\": [],\\n   510→        \\\"output\\\": [],\\n   511→        \\\"subjects\\\": subjects,\\n   512→        \\\"subject_idx\\\": np.empty(len(results), dtype=np.int16),\\n   513→        \\\"brain_regions\\\": brain_regions,\\n   514→        \\\"brain_region_idx\\\": [],\\n   515→        \\\"input_names\\\": [\\\"time_from_tone_onset_s\\\", \\\"photostim_on\\\"],\\n   516→        \\\"output_names\\\": [\\\"choice\\\", \\\"outcome\\\", \\\"early_lick\\\", \\\"tongue_y_position\\\"],\\n   517→        \\\"output_values\\\": [\\n   518→            [\\\"left\\\", \\\"right\\\"],\\n   519→            [\\\"ignore\\\", \\\"miss\\\", \\\"hit\\\"],\\n   520→            [\\\"no\\\", \\\"yes\\\"],\\n   521→            [\\\"lt_40pct\\\", \\\"40_to_60pct\\\", \\\"gt_60pct\\\"],\\n   522→        ],\\n   523→        \\\"metadata\\\": {\\n   524→            \\\"task_description\\\": (\\n   525→                \\\"Auditory delayed-response task aligned to go cue. (https://doi.org/10.25378/janelia.24066108.v1). In brief, we used 15 cluster quality metrics to train classiﬁers - one for each of the major ﬁve brain areas (cortex, striatum, thalamus, midbrain and medulla). The classiﬁers were trained based on labels derived from manual curation. We curated Kilosort2 output (28 penetrations from ﬁve brain areas) using the open-source graphical user interface Phy (GitHub - cortex-lab/phy: phy: interactive visualization and manual spike sorting of large-scale ephys data). Each cluster was labeled as a ‘good’ or ‘unlabeled’ unit. Annotators were blind to location, recording condition and trial type information. Based on the annotated labels of all clusters and associated quality metrics, we trained ﬁve region-speciﬁc classiﬁers using logistic regression. Applying the trained classiﬁers to the rest of the dataset provided lists of units that were labeled as ‘good’ and were used for the analysis in this paper. We applied the cortex classiﬁer to ALM and other cortex, hippocampus, olfactory and cortical subplate units; the striatum classiﬁer to striatum and pallidum units; the thalamus classiﬁer to thalamus and hypothalamus units; the midbrain classiﬁer to midbrain and pons units; the medulla classiﬁer to medulla and cerebellum units. We calculated the rate at which the classiﬁer incorrectly labeled ’unlabeled’ units as ’good’, using 10-fold cross-validation on held-out manually labeled units (false alarm rate): 7.8%; striatum: 6.4%; thalamus: 7.3%; midbrain: 5.5%; medulla 4.3%. This method yielded 8717 good units from ALM, 7664 from striatum, 12808 from thalamus, 7495 from midbrain and 2928 from medulla, etc. Overall, the dataset consisted of 69,943 good units recorded across 173 behavioral sessions, from which 655 probe insertions were made. This corresponds to 25.9 % of clusters reported by Kilosort2. VideoAnalysisUtils.preprocessing_utils as preprocessing_utils\\n    19→\\n    20→\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    21→Important notes on reading MATLAB files using Python:\\n    22→When we read an Nx1 MATLAB cell into a length-N Python list/array/anything iterable (and potentially do something before storing it into an iterable, e.g. if the cell contains indices, we might need to subtract 1 from each element since MATLAB counts from 1 and Python counts from 0), pay attention to the following three cases:\\n    23→1. Empty 0x1 cell. Need to manually define what it becomes in Python.\\n    24→2. 1x1 cell. Will be directly read as non-iterables such as str, int, float, etc. Need to manually convert it to an iterable with length 1.\\n    25→3. Nx1 cell (N >= 2). Usually it's fine to read and directly store it in Python.\\n    26→\\n    27→Comment: in case 2, when using isinstance(data, data_type), if data could be a number, consider the possibility of data being either float or int, i.e. use\\n    28→    if isinstance(data, float) or isinstance(data, int):\\n    29→        do something\\n    30→\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    31→\\n    32→plt.rcParams['pdf.fonttype'] = 42\\n    33→plt.rcParams['ps.fonttype'] = 42\\n    34→\\n    35→\\n    36→def sliding_histogram(spikeTimes, begin_time, end_time, bin_width, stride, rate=True):\\n    37→    '''\\n    38→    Calculates the number of spikes for each unit in each sliding bin of width bin_width, strided by stride.\\n    39→    begin_time and end_time are treated as the lower- and upper- bounds for bin centers.\\n    40→    if rate=True, calculate firing rates instead of bin spikes.\\n    41→    '''\\n    42→    bin_begin_time = begin_time\\n    43→    bin_end_time = end_time\\n    44→    # This is to deal with cases where for e.g. (bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride is actually 43 but evaluates to 42.999999... due to numerical errors in floating-point arithmetic.\\n    45→    if np.allclose((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride, math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1):\\n    46→        n_bins =  math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+2\\n    47→    else:\\n    48→        n_bins = math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1\\n    49→    binCenters = bin_begin_time + np.arange(n_bins)*stride\\n    50→\\n    51→    binIntervals = np.vstack((binCenters-bin_width/2, binCenters+bin_width/2)).T\\n    52→\\n    53→    # Count number of spikes for each sliding bin\\n    54→    binSpikes = np.asarray([[[np.sum(np.all([trial>=binInt[0], trial<binInt[1]], axis=0)) for binInt in binIntervals] for trial in unit]\\n    55→        for unit in spikeTimes]).swapaxes(0,-1)\\n    56→\\n    57→    if rate:\\n    58→        return binCenters, binSpikes/float(bin_width) # get firing rates\\n    59→    return binCenters, binSpikes\\n    60→\\n    61→\\n    62→def process_one_sess(data_folder, file_path_list, bw, stride, begin_time=-3.0, end_time=3.5, save_folder=os.path.join(os.pardir, 'data_processed'), qc_mode='classifier'):\\n    63→    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    64→    Process all probe files in one session.\\n    65→\\n    66→    Args:\\n    67→        data_folder (str): the folder containing all data including QC neuron ID files.\\n    68→        file_path_list: list of full file paths of all probes in this session.\\n    69→        bw (float): in sec.\\n    70→        stride (float): in sec.\\n    71→        qc_mode (str): 'classifier' or 'old'.\\n    72→    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    73→    print(qc_mode)\\n    74→    if qc_mode == 'classifier':\\n    75→        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'goodunits')\\n    76→        print(qc_folder)\\n    77→    elif qc_mode == 'old':\\n    78→        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'DJ_GoodUnitsIdx_14regions_old_qc')\\n    79→    else:\\n    80→        raise ValueError('Wrong qc_mode! Each element is a list of 0 (lick right) and 1 (lick left)\\n   149→            sess_dict['lick_times'] = lick_time_cal # list with length n_trials, each element is 1D ndarray with all lick times in that trial\\n   150→            sess_dict['gocue_time'] = gocue_time[0,:]\\n   151→            sess_dict['correctness'] = correctness # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64. 1: correct or free water, 0: error, -1: no response\\n   152→            sess_dict['delay_period'] = delay_period # (n_trials,) duration of delay period\\n   153→            sess_dict['sample_period'] = sample_period # (n_trials,) duration of sample period\\n   154→            sess_dict['stimulation'] = stimulation # ndarray, (n_trials,4). The 4 columns are [laser_power, stim_type('1', '2', or '6', with 1,2,6 being left/right/both ALM perturbation), laser_on_time, laser_off_time]. For no stim trials, we have [0, NaN, NaN, NaN]\\n   155→            sess_dict['trial_type'] = 1*(trial_type=='l') # ndarray, (n_trials,). 1 is left, 0 is right 'r'\\n   156→            # with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, 'behavior_task.pickle'), 'wb') as f:\\n   157→            #     pickle.dump(sess_dict, f)\\n   158→\\n   159→            ### Read the neuron IDs after QC for this session ###\\n   160→            '''\\n   161→            The index refers to within individual SESSION, resulting in summing all units from all penetrations in a given Session. An index of '42' from this session means the 2nd (42-10-30) unit from p3.\\n   162→            '''\\n   163→            temp_list = list(glob.glob(os.path.join(qc_folder, '{}*.mat'.format(sess_name_qc))))\\n   164→            assert len(temp_list) == 1\\n   165→            filepath_qc = temp_list[0]\\n   166→\\n   167→            qc_file = preprocessing_utils.loadmat(filepath_qc)\\n   168→            # idx_qc_dict = qc_file['Idx'] # dict. Process neurons from areas of interest and perform QC filtering -----\\n   250→    i_file = 1\\n   251→    neuron_id_used = []\\n   252→    neuron_id_all = np.arange(n_neurons) # neuron_id is continuous from 0. unit_id is the original id from DJ.\\n   253→\\n   254→    for region in ['ALM', 'Medulla', 'Midbrain', 'Striatum', 'Thalamus', 'Pons', 'Cerebellum', 'Hypothalamus', 'Hippocampus', 'Orbital', 'OtherCortex', 'Olfactory', 'CorticalSubplate', 'Pallidum']:\\n   255→        for side in ['left', 'right']:\\n   256→            area = '_'.join([side, region])\\n   257→            area_key = '{}_qc'.format(region)\\n   258→            neuron_id_area = helper_get_neuron_id_area(idx_qc_dict[area_key], anno_qc_dict[area_key], ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, side, region)\\n   259→\\n   260→            if len(neuron_id_area) == 0:\\n   261→                continue\\n   262→\\n   263→            print('Filtered neurons:', area, len(neuron_id_area))\\n   264→\\n   265→            # record used neurons\\n   266→            neuron_id_used = neuron_id_used + neuron_id_area\\n   267→\\n   268→            save_flag = process_one_area(sess_name, area, neuron_id_area, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, unit_qc_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict)\\n   269→\\n   270→            if save_flag:\\n   271→                i_file += 1\\n   272→\\n   273→    # ----- 4. Empty 0x1 cell. Need to manually define what it becomes in Python.\n2. 1x1 cell. Will be directly read as non-iterables such as str, int, float, etc. Need to manually convert it to an iterable with length 1.\n3. Nx1 cell (N >= 2). Usually it's fine to read and directly store it in Python.\n\nComment: in case 2, when using isinstance(data, data_type), if data could be a number, consider the possibility of data being either float or int, i.e. use\n    if isinstance(data, float) or isinstance(data, int):\n        do something\n\"\"\"\n\nplt.rcParams['pdf.fonttype'] = 42\nplt.rcParams['ps.fonttype'] = 42\n\n\ndef sliding_histogram(spikeTimes, begin_time, end_time, bin_width, stride, rate=True):\n    '''\n    Calculates the number of spikes for each unit in each sliding bin of width bin_width, strided by stride.\n    begin_time and end_time are treated as the lower- and upper- bounds for bin centers.\n    if rate=True, calculate firing rates instead of bin spikes.\n    '''\n    bin_begin_time = begin_time\n    bin_end_time = end_time\n    # This is to deal with cases where for e.g. (bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride is actually 43 but evaluates to 42.999999... due to numerical errors in floating-point arithmetic.\n    if np.allclose((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride, math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1):\n        n_bins =  math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+2\n    else:\n        n_bins = math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1\n    binCenters = bin_begin_time + np.arange(n_bins)*stride\n\n    binIntervals = np.vstack((binCenters-bin_width/2, binCenters+bin_width/2)).T\n\n    # Count number of spikes for each sliding bin\n    binSpikes = np.asarray([[[np.sum(np.all([trial>=binInt[0], trial<binInt[1]], axis=0)) for binInt in binIntervals] for trial in unit]\n        for unit in spikeTimes]).swapaxes(0,-1)\n\n    if rate:\n        return binCenters, binSpikes/float(bin_width) # The instruction stimuli during the sample epoch were pure tones played at one of two frequencies (3 kHz or 12 kHz). Each tone was played three times for 150 ms with 100 ms inter-tone intervals. The sample epoch was followed by a 1.2 s delay epoch. An auditory ‘Go’ cue (carrier frequency of 6 kHz with 360 Hz modulating fre-\nquency, 0.1 s duration) indicated the end of the delay epoch. Licking early during the sample/delay epoch triggered a replay of the epoch. During the response epoch (answer period: 1.5 s) mice reported the instruction by licking one of the two lick ports. Licking (consumption period: 1.5 s) the correct lick port triggered a small water reward (0.1 ~ 0.2 ul). Licking the incorrect lick port triggered a timeout (1-3 s). After mice stopped licking for 1.5 s, the trial ended, followed by a 250 ms inter-trial-interval. Early lick trials and no\nresponse trials were excluded for analysis. Overall performance was computed as the fraction of correct control trials (i.e. no photostimulation), excluding any early lick trials. We selected experimental sessions for analysis based on following criteria: overall behavioral performance (> 65%), and at least 50 correct lick left and lick right trials each. Mice performed on average 476 (Mean; range, 130-785) trials per session with 84% correct rate (range, 65-99%; STAR Methods). During the delay epoch, mice maintain a memory of their choice and plan a movement.\n\nTwo CMOS cameras (CM3-U3-13Y3M, FLIR) were used to track orofacial movements of the mouse under IR light illumination (940 nm LED). The cameras were equipped with 4-12 mm focal length lenses (12VM412ASIR, Tamron) and a pixel resolution of 71 mm. We trained DeepLabCut 39 to track the movement of tongue, jaw and nose (Figure 1I).\n\nMulti-regional electrophysiological recordings\n\nWe designed a ﬂexible manipulator system for multiple Neuropixels probe insertions (https://www.janelia.org/open-science/manipulator-system-for-multiple-neuropixels-probe-recordings). Three to four small craniotomies (diameter, 1 \u0018 1.5 mm) were made over target brain areas one day before the ﬁrst recording session. Photoinactivation of ALM (centered on AP 2.5 mm; ML 1.5 mm) was performed through clear-skull cap implant11 or via craniotomy by directing the laser over the skull (beam diameter, 1.8 mm at 4 sigma). Photoinhibition of the left-, right-ALM hemisphere, or both hemispheres was deployed on a subset of ~25% randomly interleaved trials 11,21 (N = 17 VGAT-ChR2-EYFP mice). To prevent the mice from distinguishing photostimulation trials from control rials using visual cues, a ‘masking ﬂash’ (1 ms pulses at 10 Hz) was delivered using 470 nm LEDs in front of the mice throughout the entire trial. For silencing ALM, we stimulated cortical GABAergic neurons in VGAT-ChR2-EYFP mice. We used 40 Hz photostimulation with a sinusoidal temporal proﬁle (5 mW average power per hemisphere) and a 100 ms linear ramping down during the laser offset to reduce rebound neuronal activity. This photo stimulus is expected to silence glutamatergic neurons in all of ALM. 37 We silenced ALM activity during the late delay epoch (last 0.5 s), including the 100 ms ramp-down. Thus, photoinhibition always ended before the ‘Go’ cue.\n\nTo analyze the effects of photoinhibition, units with at least 10 photostimulation trials were selected for analysis (Figures 5 and S5). To exclude the effects of rebound population activity in the thalamus 16 and other subcortical regions, we used a 20-120 ms\ntime window after photo stimulation onset to measure the effect of ALM inactivation in downstream regions (Figures 5 and S5). Both sorting methods gave similar results. In the analyses presented in the paper, we used the output from Kilosort2. Each output cluster was associated with a list of 15 quality metrics calculated to assess isolation and sorting quality 7 (i.e. quality control) (https://github.com/AllenInstitute/ecephys_spike_sorting/tree/master/ecephys_spike_sorting/modules/quality_metrics).\n\nThe details of the spike sorting and quality control are described in an accompanying white paper (https://doi.org/10.25378/janelia.24066108.v1). In brief, we used 15 cluster quality metrics to train classiﬁers - one for each of the major ﬁve brain areas (cortex, striatum, thalamus, midbrain and medulla). The classiﬁers were trained based on labels derived from manual curation. We curated Kilosort2 output (28 penetrations from ﬁve brain areas) using the open-source graphical user interface Phy (GitHub - cortex-lab/phy: phy: interactive visualization and manual spike sorting of large-scale ephys data). Each cluster was labeled as a ‘good’ or ‘unlabeled’ unit. Annotators were blind to location, recording condition and trial type information. Based on the annotated labels of all clusters and associated quality metrics, we trained ﬁve region-speciﬁc classiﬁers using logistic regression. Applying the trained classiﬁers to the rest of the dataset provided lists of units that were labeled as ‘good’ and were used for the analysis in this paper. We applied the cortex classiﬁer to ALM and other cortex, hippocampus, olfactory and cortical subplate units; the striatum classiﬁer to striatum and pallidum units; the thalamus classiﬁer to thalamus and hypothalamus units; the midbrain classiﬁer to midbrain and pons units; the medulla classiﬁer to medulla and cerebellum units.
